      \        85.55853271484375,\n        37.97945785522461,\n        520.3282470703125,\n        790.9714965820312\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"42.2 \\nOccupational Health Centre  \\n42.2.1 \\\
      nThe contractor shall ensure at a construction site an occupational health centre, \\nmobile or static is\
      \ provided and maintained in good order. Services and facilities \\nas per the scale lay down in Schedule\
      \ X of BOCWR. A construction medical \\nofficer appointed in an occupational health centre, possess the qualification\
      \ as \\nlaid down in Schedule XI of BOCWR. \\n \\n42.3 \\nAmbulance van and room \\n \\n42.3.1 \\nThe contractor\
      \ shall ensure at a construction site of a building or other \\nconstruction work that an ambulance van and\
      \ room are provided at such \\nconstruction site or an arrangement is made with a nearby hospital for providing\
      \ \\nsuch ambulance van for transportation of serious cases of accident or sickness \\nof workers to hospital\
      \ promptly and such ambulance van and room are \\nmaintained in good repair and is equipped with standard\
      \ facilities specified in \\nSchedule IV and Schedule V of BOCWR. \\n \\n42.4 \\nFirst-aid boxes \\n \\n42.4.1\
      \ \\nThe contractor shall ensure at a construction site one First-aid box for 100 workers \\nprovided and\
      \ maintained for providing First-aid to the building workers. Every First-aid \\nbox is distinctly marked\
      \ “First-aid” and is equipped with the articles specified in \\nSchedule III of BOCWR. \\n \\n42.5 \\nHIV/\
      \ AIDS prevention and control \\n \\n42.5.1 \\nThe contractor shall adopt the Employer’s Policy on “HIV /\
      \ AIDS Prevention and \\nControl for Workmen Engaged by Contractors” and the copy of the policy is given in\
      \ \\nAppendix No.: 4.  \\n \\n42.5.2 \\nThe Employer will engage a professional agency for implementing the\
      \ guidelines laid \\ndown in the policy and communicate to the contractor. \\n \\n42.5.3 \\nThe Contractor\
      \ shall extend necessary support to the appointed agency by deputing \\nthe workmen to attend the awareness\
      \ creation programmes. \\n \\n42.5.4 \\nThe contractor shall also extend necessary organizational support\
      \ to the appointed \\nagency for the effective implementation of the Employers’ workplace policy on \\nHIV/AIDS\
      \ for workmen of the Contractors. \\n \\n42.5.5 \\nAs laid down in the policy the contractor shall identify\
      \ peer educators (1 for every 100 \\nworkers) and refer them for professional training to the Employers’ appointed\
      \ agency \\nfor the purpose. \\n \\n42.5.6 \\nThe peer educators on completion of the training shall serve\
      \ as the focal point \\nfor any information, education and awareness campaign among the workmen \\nthroughout\
      \ the contract period. \\n \\n42.5.7 \\nThe peer educators will be paid a monthly honorarium as fixed by the\
      \ Employer for \\nrendering his services in addition to his regular duty. \\n \\n42.5.8 \\nThe total number\
      \ of peer educators (1 for 100 workers) shall always be maintained by \\nthe contractor. \\n \\n \\n42.5.9\
      \ \\nIn case if these peer educators leave the contractor by creating vacancy, then \\nthe contractor at his\
      \ own expense train the new replacement peer educator from \\nthe Employers’ appointed agency for the purpose.\
      \ \\n42.5.10 \\nIt is suggested to the contractor that due care should be taken to select the peer \\neducators\
      \ from among the group of workmen so that they remain with the contractor \\nthroughout the contract period.\
      \ \\n42.6 \\nPrevention of mosquito breeding \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        39.599998474121094,\n\
      \        24.479991912841797,\n        63.57600021362305,\n        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 4,\n      \"text\": \"249\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n}"

- kind: parameter
  text: In free tunnels, fresh air supply must not be less than 6 m3/min per underground building worker, free air
    movement must be at least 9 m/min, and oxygen levels must not fall below 19.5%.
  evidence_ids:
  - J79c6bd3eb032dd81
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: Cfd28e2aba82aa591
  citations:
  - id: J79c6bd3eb032dd81
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part3.md
    okf_sha256: b973183de77a23a5c4e6f7df14a6bfe6e19a3add3e80a6dc1db238991aff8dd3
    heading: Clause 44.0 — Ventilation and illumination
    source_document: Vol3DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 1ee3fc8dfd12f659f34446b0a6db74e1e1cdd4d593ca91b2a48d59bf1ef97d52
    pdf_pages:
    - 70
    - 71
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol3/part_3_health_welfare/health_welfare_clauses.json
    json_sha256: dc172436c564fe7d5a449f7f786e6863b16e68ebd59020d555002ab6fc04758d
    json_pointer: /clauses/3
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"44.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 70,\n      \"text\": \"\
      44.0      Ventilation and illumination\\n\\n\\n44.1      Ventilation\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 71,\n      \"text\": \"44.1.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction site of a building or other\
      \ construction\\n                 work that all working areas in a free tunnel are provided with ventilation\
      \ system as\\n                 approved by the DG and the fresh air supply in such tunnel is not less than\
      \ 6m3/min\\n                       for each building worker employed underground in such tunnel and the free\
      \ air flow\\n                movement inside such tunnel is not less than 9m/min.\\n\\n\\n          44.1.2\
      \   The oxygen level shall not be less than 19.5% in the working environment.\\n\\n\\n          44.2     \
      \ Illumination\\n\\n          44.2.1   The contractor shall take every effort to illuminate the work site\
      \ as per the Employer’s\\n                   requirement illustrated in general instruction BMRC/SHE/CEO/011.\\\
      n\\n          44.2.2   The contractor shall conduct a monthly illumination monitoring by lux meter for\\n\
      \                         all the locations and the report shall be sent to the Employer within 7th of the\\\
      n                  next month and the same shall be reviewed during the monthly SHE committee\\n         \
      \         meeting.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"44.0      Ventilation and illumination\",\n  \"retained_lines\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 70,\n      \"source_line_number\": 73,\n      \"text\": \"44.0      Ventilation\
      \ and illumination\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 70,\n      \"source_line_number\": 74,\n      \"\
      text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 70,\n      \"source_line_number\": 75,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 70,\n      \"source_line_number\": 76,\n      \"text\": \"44.1    \
      \  Ventilation\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 70,\n      \"source_line_number\": 77,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 70,\n      \"source_line_number\": 78,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 70,\n      \"source_line_number\": 79,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 70,\n      \"source_line_number\": 80,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 70,\n      \"source_line_number\": 81,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 71,\n      \"source_line_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"          44.1.1   The contractor shall\
      \ ensure at a construction site of a building or other construction\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\":\
      \ 71,\n      \"source_line_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"                 work that all working areas in\
      \ a free tunnel are provided with ventilation system as\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 71,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"                 approved by the DG and the fresh air supply in\
      \ such tunnel is not less than 6m3/min\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 71,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 6,\n      \"text\": \"                       for each building worker employed underground in such tunnel\
      \ and the free air flow\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 71,\n      \"source_line_number\": 7,\n    \
      \  \"text\": \"                movement inside such tunnel is not less than 9m/min.\"\n    },\n    {\n   \
      \   \"pdf_page\": 71,\n      \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 71,\n      \"source_line_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 71,\n  \
      \    \"source_line_number\": 10,\n      \"text\": \"          44.1.2   The oxygen level shall not be less\
      \ than 19.5% in the working environment.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 71,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 11,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 71,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n \

      : 14,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 71,\n      \"source_line_number\": 15,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"          44.2.1   The contractor shall take every effort to illuminate the work site as\
      \ per the Employer’s\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 71,\n      \"source_line_number\": 16,\n      \"\
      text\": \"                   requirement illustrated in general instruction BMRC/SHE/CEO/011.\"\n    },\n\

      \      \"pdf_page\": 71,\n      \"source_line_number\": 18,\n      \"text\": \"          44.2.2   The contractor\

      \      \"source_line_number\": 19,\n      \"text\": \"                         all the locations and the report\

- kind: parameter
  text: Drinking water provisions require a minimum supply of 5 litres per workman per day, located within 200m
    of all work locations, and situated within six meters of washing places, urinals, or latrines.
  evidence_ids:
  - Jeeb17043f0400cc9
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"46.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 71,\n      \"text\": \"\
      46.0     Welfare measures for workers\\n\\n\\n          46.1      Latrine and Urinal Accommodation\\n\\n\\\
      n          46.1.1   The contractor shall provide one latrine seat for every 20 workers up to 100\\n      \
      \           workers and thereafter one for every additional 50 workers. In addition one\\n               \
      \     urinal accommodation shall be provided for every 100 workers.\\n\\n\\n          46.1.2   When women\
      \ are employed, separate latrine and urinals accommodation shall be\\n                   provided on the same\
      \ scale as mentioned above.\\n\\n\\n          46.1.3    Latrine and urinals shall be provided as per Section\
      \ 33 of BOCWA and maintained as\\n                   per Rule 243 of BOCWR and shall also comply with the\
      \ requirements of public health\\n                      authorities\\n\\n\\n          46.1.4   Moving sites\\\
      n\\n\\n          46.1.4.1  In case of works like track laying, the zone of work is constantly moving at elevated\\\
      n                      level or at underground level. In such cases mobile toilets with proper facility to\
      \ drain\\n                    the sullage shall be provided at reasonably accessible distance.\\n\\n\\n  \
      \        46.1.5    In case if the contractor fail to provide required number of urinals and latrines\\n  \
      \                or fail to maintain it as per the requirements of Public Health laws, the Employer\"\n  \
      \  },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 72,\n      \"text\": \"shall have the right to provide/maintain through\
      \ renowned external agencies\\n           like “Sulabh” at the cost of the contractor.\\n\\n\\n46.2     Canteen\\\
      n\\n\\n46.2.1    In every workplace wherein not less than 250 workers are ordinarily employed\\n         the\
      \ contractor shall provide an adequate canteen conforming to Section 37 of\\n       BOCWA, Rule 244 of BOCWR\
      \ and as stipulated in Rule 247 of BOCWR the\\n        changes for food stuff shall be based on ‘no profit\
      \ no loss’ basis. The price list\\n          of all items shall be conspicuously displayed in such canteen.\\\
      n\\n\\n46.3     Serving of tea and snacks at the workplace\\n\\n\\n46.3.1   As per Rule 246 of BOCWR, at a\
      \ building or other construction work where a\\n        workplace is situated at a distance of more than 200\
      \ m from the canteen\\n         provided under Rule 244(1) of BOCWR, the contractor employing building works\\\
      n          shall make suitable arrangement for serving tea and light refreshment to such\\n         building\
      \ works at such place.\\n\\n46.4     Drinking water\\n\\n\\n46.4.1   As per Section 32 of BOCWA the contractor\
      \ shall make in every worksite,\\n          effective arrangements to provide sufficient supply of wholesome\
      \ drinking\\n         water with minimum quantity of 5 litres per workman per day. Quality of the\\n     \
      \    drinking water shall conform to the requirements of national standards on\\n         Public Health.\\\
      n\\n\\n46.4.2   While locating these drinking water facility due care shall be taken so that these are\\n\
      \           easily accessible within a distance of 200m from the place of work for all workers\\n        \
      \   at all location of work sites.\\n\\n\\n46.4.3     All such points shall be legible marked “Drinking Water”\
      \ in a language understood by a\\n          majority of the workmen employed in such place and such point\
      \ shall be situated\\n           within six metres of any washing places, urinals or latrines.\\n\\n\\n46.5\
      \     Labour Accommodation\\n\\n\\n46.5.1   The contractor shall provide free of charges as near as possible,\
      \ temporary\\n          living accommodation to all workers conforming to provisions of Section 34 of\\n \
      \      BOCWA. These accommodations shall have cooking place, bathing, washing\\n        and lavatory facilities\\\
      n\\n46.6     Creche\\n\\n46.6.1    In every workplace where in more than 50 female workers are ordinarily\
      \ employed,\\n          there shall be provided and maintained a suitable room for use of children under age\\\
      n           of 6 yrs, conforming to the provisions of Section 35 of BOCWA.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\"\

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"46.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 71,\n      \"text\": \"\
      46.0     Welfare measures for workers\\n\\n\\n          46.1      Latrine and Urinal Accommodation\\n\\n\\\
      n          46.1.1   The contractor shall provide one latrine seat for every 20 workers up to 100\\n      \
      \           workers and thereafter one for every additional 50 workers. In addition one\\n               \
      \     urinal accommodation shall be provided for every 100 workers.\\n\\n\\n          46.1.2   When women\
      \ are employed, separate latrine and urinals accommodation shall be\\n                   provided on the same\
      \ scale as mentioned above.\\n\\n\\n          46.1.3    Latrine and urinals shall be provided as per Section\
      \ 33 of BOCWA and maintained as\\n                   per Rule 243 of BOCWR and shall also comply with the\
      \ requirements of public health\\n                      authorities\\n\\n\\n          46.1.4   Moving sites\\\
      n\\n\\n          46.1.4.1  In case of works like track laying, the zone of work is constantly moving at elevated\\\
      n                      level or at underground level. In such cases mobile toilets with proper facility to\
      \ drain\\n                    the sullage shall be provided at reasonably accessible distance.\\n\\n\\n  \
      \        46.1.5    In case if the contractor fail to provide required number of urinals and latrines\\n  \
      \                or fail to maintain it as per the requirements of Public Health laws, the Employer\"\n  \
      \  },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 72,\n      \"text\": \"shall have the right to provide/maintain through\
      \ renowned external agencies\\n           like “Sulabh” at the cost of the contractor.\\n\\n\\n46.2     Canteen\\\
      n\\n\\n46.2.1    In every workplace wherein not less than 250 workers are ordinarily employed\\n         the\
      \ contractor shall provide an adequate canteen conforming to Section 37 of\\n       BOCWA, Rule 244 of BOCWR\
      \ and as stipulated in Rule 247 of BOCWR the\\n        changes for food stuff shall be based on ‘no profit\
      \ no loss’ basis. The price list\\n          of all items shall be conspicuously displayed in such canteen.\\\
      n\\n\\n46.3     Serving of tea and snacks at the workplace\\n\\n\\n46.3.1   As per Rule 246 of BOCWR, at a\
      \ building or other construction work where a\\n        workplace is situated at a distance of more than 200\
      \ m from the canteen\\n         provided under Rule 244(1) of BOCWR, the contractor employing building works\\\
      n          shall make suitable arrangement for serving tea and light refreshment to such\\n         building\
      \ works at such place.\\n\\n46.4     Drinking water\\n\\n\\n46.4.1   As per Section 32 of BOCWA the contractor\
      \ shall make in every worksite,\\n          effective arrangements to provide sufficient supply of wholesome\
      \ drinking\\n         water with minimum quantity of 5 litres per workman per day. Quality of the\\n     \
      \    drinking water shall conform to the requirements of national standards on\\n         Public Health.\\\
      n\\n\\n46.4.2   While locating these drinking water facility due care shall be taken so that these are\\n\
      \           easily accessible within a distance of 200m from the place of work for all workers\\n        \
      \   at all location of work sites.\\n\\n\\n46.4.3     All such points shall be legible marked “Drinking Water”\
      \ in a language understood by a\\n          majority of the workmen employed in such place and such point\
      \ shall be situated\\n           within six metres of any washing places, urinals or latrines.\\n\\n\\n46.5\
      \     Labour Accommodation\\n\\n\\n46.5.1   The contractor shall provide free of charges as near as possible,\
      \ temporary\\n          living accommodation to all workers conforming to provisions of Section 34 of\\n \
      \      BOCWA. These accommodations shall have cooking place, bathing, washing\\n        and lavatory facilities\\\
      n\\n46.6     Creche\\n\\n46.6.1    In every workplace where in more than 50 female workers are ordinarily\
      \ employed,\\n          there shall be provided and maintained a suitable room for use of children under age\\\
      n           of 6 yrs, conforming to the provisions of Section 35 of BOCWA.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\"\
      : \"          46.0     Welfare measures for workers\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"page_rect\": [\n\
      \    0.0,\n    0.0,\n    595.219970703125,\n    842.0\n  ],\n  \"pdf_page\": 68,\n  \"review_status\": \"\
      pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\\n  \
      \                                                        252\\n43.2.10  The Engineer shall monitor Contractor’s\
      \ performance of tasks specified, and  will\\n          inspect necessary records, reports and procedures\
      \ related to the control of noise.\\n\\n43.2.11  Impact monitoring shall be carried out at noise sensitive\
      \ receptor locations within 200\\n           feet of the construction site once each week and after a change\
      \ in construction\\n            activity. Construction noise measurements shall coincide with daytime and\
      \ nighttime\\n          periods of maximum noise generating construction activities.\\n\\n43.2.12  Noise Monitoring\
      \ data will be submitted in a Noise Measurement Report Form. It will\\n          contain the type of measurement,\
      \ duration of measurement, distance of monitoring\\n         from construction site, and construction equipment\
      \ working during monitoring period.\\n\\n43.2.13   appropriate  parameter  for measuring  construction  noise\
      \  impacts  shall be  the\\n          equivalent A-weighted sound pressure level (Leq) measured in decibels\
      \ (dB). The two\\n            statistical sound levels L10 and L90; the level exceeded for 10 and 90 percent\
      \ of the time\\n           respectively, shall also be recorded during monitoring. The L90 may be considered\
      \ as\\n          the ambient level into which the L10 as average peak level intrudes. The Lmax, Leq, L10\\\
      n        and L90 values will be reported in the noise measurement form along with allowable\\n         noise\
      \ limit. The duration of monitoring shall be for a minimum of 30 minutes.\\n\\n43.2..14  In no case  shall\
      \ the Contractor expose the public  to construction noise levels\\n         exceeding 90dBA (slow) or to impulsive\
      \ noise levels with a peak sound pressure level\\n         exceeding 140dB as measured on an impulse sound\
      \ level meter.\\n\\n43.2.15   Limit for construction noise is based on the existing ambient noise levels in\
      \ areas\\n          adjoining the construction sites.\\n\\n43.2.16 The noise levels emanating from any source\
      \ during construction, shall not exceed 5\\n         dB(A) or more above existing ambient pre-construction\
      \ noise levels when measured at\\n        a point outside the premises of the location of source. The same\
      \ may be varied from\\n         time to time by and at the sole discretion of the Engineer.\\n43.2.17  Where\
      \ there are no ambient noise measurements, the construction activities shall be\\n           limited to levels\
      \ measured at a distance of 200 feet from the construction limits or at\\n          the nearest affected building,\
      \ whichever is closer, as given in Table-2.\\n                                 TABLE-2\\n                \
      \  ALLOWABLE CONSTRUCTION NOISE\\n     LAND USE               MAXIMUM NOISE LEVELS- Lmax dB (A)\\n       \
      \                                     Day Time Night Time\\n          Residential                      75\
      \             65\\n          At all Times\\n          Commercial                                   85\\n \
      \            Industrial                                     90\\n\\n\\n43.2.18   At the surface of the construction\
      \ site during night time hours, the Contractor shall use\\n          only equipment that operating under full\
      \ load meets the noise limits specified in Table-\\n           3, if a sensitive receptor would be affected.\\\
      n                             TABLE-3\\n NOISE EMISSION LIMITS FOR CONSTRUCTION EQUIPMENT USED DURING NIGHTTIME\\\
      n              HOURS; MEASURED AT 50 FEET FROM CONSTRUCTION EQUIPMENT*\\n Equipment Category             \
      \                        Lmax Level dB(A)\\n\\n Backhoe                                                80\\\
      n Bar Bender                                              75\\n Chain Saw                                \
      \              81\\n Compactor                                              80\\n Compressor             \
      \                                80\\n Concrete Mixer                                           85\\n Concrete\
      \ Pump                                           82\\n Crane                                             \
      \     85\\n Dozer                                                  85\\n Front End Loader                \
      \                         80\\n Generator                                               82\\n Gradall    \
      \                                             85\\n Grader                                               \
      \   85\\n Paver                                                  85\\n\\n\\n\\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation\
      \ Ltd.              September 2026     Section Page 64 of 114\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        47.039981842041016,\n        15.768376350402832,\n        561.0993041992188,\n        24.210372924804688\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \"                                                \
      \                                                                                   Safety Health & Environment\
      \ (SHE) Manual \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.4399642944336,\n        26.57955551147461,\n\
      \        85.20714569091797,\n        35.93199157714844\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.4399642944336,\n        809.042724609375,\n     \
      \   508.6270751953125,\n        830.5714111328125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \"\
      Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                              September 2026         Section Page 64\
      \ of 114 \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.43956756591797,\n        38.09945297241211,\n\
      \        512.7686767578125,\n        421.1317443847656\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"43.2.10 \\nThe Engineer shall monitor Contractor’s performance of tasks specified, and will \\ninspect\
      \ necessary records, reports and procedures related to the control of noise. \\n \\n43.2.11 \\nImpact monitoring\
      \ shall be carried out at noise sensitive receptor locations within 200 \\nfeet of the construction site once\
      \ each week and after a change in construction \\nactivity. Construction noise measurements shall coincide\
      \ with daytime and nighttime \\nperiods of maximum noise generating construction activities. \\n \\n43.2.12\
      \ \\nNoise Monitoring data will be submitted in a Noise Measurement Report Form. It will \\ncontain the type\
      \ of measurement, duration of measurement, distance of monitoring \\nfrom construction site, and construction\
      \ equipment working during monitoring period. \\n \\n43.2.13    appropriate parameter for measuring construction\
      \ noise impacts shall be the \\nequivalent A-weighted sound pressure level (Leq) measured in decibels (dB).\
      \  The two \\nstatistical sound levels L10 and L90; the level exceeded for 10 and 90 percent of the time \\\
      nrespectively, shall also be recorded during monitoring.  The L90 may be considered as \\nthe ambient level\
      \ into which the L10 as average peak level intrudes. The Lmax, Leq, L10 \\nand L90 values will be reported\
      \ in the noise measurement form along with allowable \\nnoise limit.  The duration of monitoring shall be\
      \ for a minimum of 30 minutes.   \\n \\n43.2..14 In no case shall the Contractor expose the public to construction\
      \ noise levels \\nexceeding 90dBA (slow) or to impulsive noise levels with a peak sound pressure level \\\
      nexceeding 140dB as measured on an impulse sound level meter.   \\n \\n \\n43.2.15 \\nLimit for construction\
      \ noise is based on the existing ambient noise levels in areas \\nadjoining the construction sites. \\n \\\
      n43.2.16  The noise levels emanating from any source during construction, shall not exceed 5 \\ndB(A) or more\
      \ above existing ambient pre-construction noise levels when measured at \\na point outside the premises of\
      \ the location of source. The same may be varied from \\ntime to time by and at the sole discretion of the\
      \ Engineer.  \\n43.2.17   Where there are no ambient noise measurements, the construction activities shall\
      \ be \\nlimited to levels measured at a distance of 200 feet from the construction limits or at \\nthe nearest\
      \ affected building, whichever is closer, as given in Table-2.   \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        82.43881225585938,\n        423.1892395019531,\n        512.7671508789062,\n        586.2517700195312\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"TABLE-2 \\nALLOWABLE CONSTRUCTION NOISE \\n \\nLAND\
      \ USE \\n \\n \\n   MAXIMUM NOISE LEVELS- Lmax dB (A) \\n                                                \
      \              Day Time Night Time \\n \\n    Residential \\n \\n \\n              75 \\n \\n    65 \\n  \
      \   At all Times \\n      Commercial                                         \\n \\n    85 \\n         \\\
      n      Industrial \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n    90 \\n     \\n \\n43.2.18     At the surface of the construction\
      \ site during night time hours, the Contractor shall use \\nonly equipment that operating under full load\
      \ meets the noise limits specified in Table-\\n3, if a sensitive receptor would be affected. \\nTABLE-3 \\\
      nNOISE EMISSION LIMITS FOR CONSTRUCTION EQUIPMENT USED DURING   NIGHTTIME \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        87.83995819091797,\n        588.4292602539062,\n        508.44769287109375,\n        610.6116943359375\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"HOURS; MEASURED AT 50 FEET FROM CONSTRUCTION EQUIPMENT*\
      \ \\nEquipment Category\\nLmax Level dB(A)\\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        87.83995819091797,\n\
      \        616.2592163085938,\n        403.2066955566406,\n        781.6114501953125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 6,\n      \"text\": \"Backhoe \\n80 \\nBar Bender \\n75 \\nChain Saw \\n81 \\nCompactor \\n80 \\nCompressor\
      \  \\n80 \\nConcrete Mixer \\n85 \\nConcrete Pump \\n82 \\nCrane \\n85 \\nDozer \\n85 \\nFront End Loader\
      \ \\n80 \\nGenerator \\n82 \\nGradall \\n85 \\nGrader \\n85 \\nPaver \\n85 \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      bbox\": [\n        546.0439453125,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        570.0199584960938,\n        41.38799285888672\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 7,\n      \"text\": \"252\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n}"

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"page_rect\": [\n\
      \    0.0,\n    0.0,\n    595.219970703125,\n    842.0\n  ],\n  \"pdf_page\": 72,\n  \"review_status\": \"\
      pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\\n  \
      \                                                        256\\n          shall have the right to provide/maintain\
      \ through renowned external agencies\\n           like “Sulabh” at the cost of the contractor.\\n\\n\\n46.2\
      \     Canteen\\n\\n\\n46.2.1    In every workplace wherein not less than 250 workers are ordinarily employed\\\
      n         the contractor shall provide an adequate canteen conforming to Section 37 of\\n       BOCWA, Rule\
      \ 244 of BOCWR and as stipulated in Rule 247 of BOCWR the\\n        changes for food stuff shall be based\
      \ on ‘no profit no loss’ basis. The price list\\n          of all items shall be conspicuously displayed in\
      \ such canteen.\\n\\n\\n46.3     Serving of tea and snacks at the workplace\\n\\n\\n46.3.1   As per Rule 246\
      \ of BOCWR, at a building or other construction work where a\\n        workplace is situated at a distance\
      \ of more than 200 m from the canteen\\n         provided under Rule 244(1) of BOCWR, the contractor employing\
      \ building works\\n          shall make suitable arrangement for serving tea and light refreshment to such\\\
      n         building works at such place.\\n\\n46.4     Drinking water\\n\\n\\n46.4.1   As per Section 32 of\
      \ BOCWA the contractor shall make in every worksite,\\n          effective arrangements to provide sufficient\
      \ supply of wholesome drinking\\n         water with minimum quantity of 5 litres per workman per day. Quality\
      \ of the\\n         drinking water shall conform to the requirements of national standards on\\n         Public\
      \ Health.\\n\\n\\n46.4.2   While locating these drinking water facility due care shall be taken so that these\
      \ are\\n           easily accessible within a distance of 200m from the place of work for all workers\\n \
      \          at all location of work sites.\\n\\n\\n46.4.3     All such points shall be legible marked “Drinking\
      \ Water” in a language understood by a\\n          majority of the workmen employed in such place and such\
      \ point shall be situated\\n           within six metres of any washing places, urinals or latrines.\\n\\\
      n\\n46.5     Labour Accommodation\\n\\n\\n46.5.1   The contractor shall provide free of charges as near as\
      \ possible, temporary\\n          living accommodation to all workers conforming to provisions of Section\
      \ 34 of\\n       BOCWA. These accommodations shall have cooking place, bathing, washing\\n        and lavatory\
      \ facilities\\n\\n46.6     Creche\\n\\n46.6.1    In every workplace where in more than 50 female workers are\
      \ ordinarily employed,\\n          there shall be provided and maintained a suitable room for use of children\
      \ under age\\n           of 6 yrs, conforming to the provisions of Section 35 of BOCWA.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\\nBangalore\
      \ Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.              September 2026     Section Page 68 of 114\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        47.039981842041016,\n        15.768376350402832,\n        561.0993041992188,\n\
      \        24.210372924804688\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \"                    \
      \                                                                                                        \
      \       Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.4399642944336,\n\
      \        26.57955551147461,\n        85.20714569091797,\n        35.93199157714844\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 1,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.4399642944336,\n        809.042724609375,\n\
      \        508.6270751953125,\n        830.5714111328125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                              September 2026         Section Page\
      \ 68 of 114 \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        127.43994903564453,\n        37.989418029785156,\n\
      \        512.7672729492188,\n        58.85191345214844\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"shall have the right to provide/maintain through renowned external agencies \\nlike “Sulabh” at the cost\
      \ of the contractor. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.4399642944336,\n        63.879310607910156,\n\
      \        169.6872100830078,\n        86.69187927246094\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" \\n46.2 \\nCanteen \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.4399642944336,\n        91.7193374633789,\n\
      \        512.7711181640625,\n        791.0919799804688\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" \\n46.2.1  \\nIn every workplace wherein not less than 250 workers are ordinarily employed \\nthe contractor\
      \ shall provide an adequate canteen conforming to Section 37 of \\nBOCWA, Rule 244 of BOCWR and as stipulated\
      \ in Rule 247 of BOCWR the \\nchanges for food stuff shall be based on ‘no profit no loss’ basis. The price\
      \ list \\nof all items shall be conspicuously displayed in such canteen. \\n \\n46.3 \\nServing of tea and\
      \ snacks at the workplace \\n \\n46.3.1  \\nAs per Rule 246 of BOCWR, at a building or other construction\
      \ work where a \\nworkplace is situated at a distance of more than 200 m from the canteen \\nprovided under\
      \ Rule 244(1) of BOCWR, the contractor employing building works \\nshall make suitable arrangement for serving\
      \ tea and light refreshment to such \\nbuilding works at such place.  \\n46.4 \\nDrinking water \\n \\n46.4.1\
      \ \\nAs per Section 32 of BOCWA the contractor shall make in every worksite, \\neffective arrangements to\
      \ provide sufficient supply of wholesome drinking \\nwater with minimum quantity of 5 litres per workman per\
      \ day. Quality of the \\ndrinking water shall conform to the requirements of national standards on \\nPublic\
      \ Health. \\n \\n46.4.2 \\nWhile locating these drinking water facility due care shall be taken so that these\
      \ are \\neasily accessible within a distance of 200m from the place of work for all workers \\nat all location\
      \ of work sites. \\n \\n46.4.3 \\nAll such points shall be legible marked “Drinking Water” in a language understood\
      \ by a \\nmajority of the workmen employed in such place and such point shall be situated \\nwithin six metres\
      \ of any washing places, urinals or latrines.  \\n \\n46.5 \\nLabour Accommodation \\n \\n46.5.1 \\nThe contractor\
      \ shall provide free of charges as near as possible, temporary \\nliving accommodation to all workers conforming\
      \ to provisions of Section 34 of \\nBOCWA. These accommodations shall have cooking place, bathing, washing\
      \ \\nand lavatory facilities \\n46.6 \\nCreche \\n46.6.1  \\nIn every workplace where in more than 50 female\
      \ workers are ordinarily employed, \\nthere shall be provided and maintained a suitable room for use of children\
      \ under age \\nof 6 yrs, conforming to the provisions of Section 35 of BOCWA.  \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\\
      n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        546.0439453125,\n        24.479991912841797,\n\
      \        570.0199584960938,\n        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\"\

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"page_rect\": [\n\

      pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\\n255\\\
      n          44.1.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction site of a building or other construction\\\
      n                 work that all working areas in a free tunnel are provided with ventilation system as\\n\
      \                 approved by the DG and the fresh air supply in such tunnel is not less than 6m3/min\\n \
      \                      for each building worker employed underground in such tunnel and the free air flow\\\
      n                movement inside such tunnel is not less than 9m/min.\\n\\n\\n          44.1.2   The oxygen\
      \ level shall not be less than 19.5% in the working environment.\\n\\n\\n          44.2      Illumination\\\
      n\\n          44.2.1   The contractor shall take every effort to illuminate the work site as per the Employer’s\\\
      n                   requirement illustrated in general instruction BMRC/SHE/CEO/011.\\n\\n          44.2.2\
      \   The contractor shall conduct a monthly illumination monitoring by lux meter for\\n                   \
      \      all the locations and the report shall be sent to the Employer within 7th of the\\n               \
      \   next month and the same shall be reviewed during the monthly SHE committee\\n                  meeting.\\\
      n\\n\\n          45.0     Radiation\\n\\n\\n          45.1    The use of radioactive substances and radiating\
      \ apparatus shall comply with the Govt.\\n                    regulatory requirements and all subsidiary legislation\\\
      n\\n\\n          45.2     Operations involving ionising radiation shall only be carried out after having been\\\
      n                  reviewed without objection by the Employers representative and shall be carried out in\\\
      n                  accordance with a method statement.\\n\\n\\n          45.3     each area containing irradiated\
      \ apparatus shall have warning notices and barriers, as\\n                    required by the Regulations,\
      \ conspicuously posted at or near the area.\\n\\n\\n          45.4     Radioactive substances will be stored,\
      \ used or disposed shall be strictly in accordance\\n                     with the Govt. Enactments.\\n\\\
      n\\n          45.5    The contractor shall ensure that all site personnel and members of the public are not\\\
      n                 exposed to radiation.\\n\\n\\n          46.0     Welfare measures for workers\\n\\n\\n \
      \         46.1      Latrine and Urinal Accommodation\\n\\n\\n          46.1.1   The contractor shall provide\
      \ one latrine seat for every 20 workers up to 100\\n                 workers and thereafter one for every\
      \ additional 50 workers. In addition one\\n                    urinal accommodation shall be provided for\
      \ every 100 workers.\\n\\n\\n          46.1.2   When women are employed, separate latrine and urinals accommodation\
      \ shall be\\n                   provided on the same scale as mentioned above.\\n\\n\\n          46.1.3  \
      \  Latrine and urinals shall be provided as per Section 33 of BOCWA and maintained as\\n                 \
      \  per Rule 243 of BOCWR and shall also comply with the requirements of public health\\n                 \
      \     authorities\\n\\n\\n          46.1.4   Moving sites\\n\\n\\n          46.1.4.1  In case of works like\
      \ track laying, the zone of work is constantly moving at elevated\\n                      level or at underground\
      \ level. In such cases mobile toilets with proper facility to drain\\n                    the sullage shall\
      \ be provided at reasonably accessible distance.\\n\\n\\n          46.1.5    In case if the contractor fail\
      \ to provide required number of urinals and latrines\\n                  or fail to maintain it as per the\
      \ requirements of Public Health laws, the Employer\\n\\n\\n\\n         Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.\
      \              September 2026     Section Page 67 of 114\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"bbox\":\
      \ [\n        50.15998077392578,\n        15.768376350402832,\n        564.21923828125,\n        24.210372924804688\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \"                                                \
      \                                                                                   Safety Health & Environment\
      \ (SHE) Manual \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.5599594116211,\n        26.57955551147461,\n\

      \ Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                              September 2026         Section Page 67 of 114 \\\
      n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.55896759033203,\n        38.09945297241211,\n      \
      \  515.8885498046875,\n        226.3718719482422\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"\
      44.1.1 \\nThe contractor shall ensure at a construction site of a building or other construction \\nwork that\
      \ all working areas in a free tunnel are provided with ventilation system as \\napproved by the DG and the\
      \ fresh air supply in such tunnel is not less than 6m3/min \\nfor each building worker employed underground\
      \ in such tunnel and the free air flow \\nmovement inside such tunnel is not less than 9m/min. \\n \\n44.1.2\
      \ \\nThe oxygen level shall not be less than 19.5% in the working environment. \\n \\n44.2  \\nIllumination\
      \ \\n44.2.1  \\nThe contractor shall take every effort to illuminate the work site as per the Employer’s \\\
      nrequirement illustrated in general instruction BMRC/SHE/CEO/011. \\n44.2.2 \\nThe contractor shall conduct\
      \ a monthly illumination monitoring by lux meter for \\nall the locations and the report shall be sent to\
      \ the Employer within 7th of the \\nnext month and the same shall be reviewed during the monthly SHE committee\
      \ \\nmeeting. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.5599594116211,\n        231.02713012695312,\n\
      \        178.92880249023438,\n        253.01182556152344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" \\n45.0 \\nRadiation \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.55854797363281,\n        257.77911376953125,\n\
      \        515.8870239257812,\n        492.6517639160156\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\"\
      : \" \\n45.1 \\nThe use of radioactive substances and radiating apparatus shall comply with the Govt. \\nregulatory\
      \ requirements and all subsidiary legislation \\n \\n45.2 \\nOperations involving ionising radiation shall\
      \ only be carried out after having been \\nreviewed without objection by the Employers representative and\
      \ shall be carried out in \\naccordance with a method statement. \\n \\n45.3 \\neach area containing irradiated\
      \ apparatus shall have warning notices and barriers, as \\nrequired by the Regulations, conspicuously posted\
      \ at or near the area. \\n \\n45.4 \\nRadioactive substances will be stored, used or disposed shall be strictly\
      \ in accordance \\nwith the Govt. Enactments. \\n \\n45.5 \\nThe contractor shall ensure that all site personnel\
      \ and members of the public are not \\nexposed to radiation.   \\n \\n46.0 \\nWelfare measures for workers\
      \ \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.55965423583984,\n        497.55914306640625,\n     \
      \   515.887451171875,\n        665.0916137695312\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \ \\n46.1 \\nLatrine and Urinal Accommodation \\n \\n46.1.1 \\nThe contractor shall provide one latrine seat\
      \ for every 20 workers up to 100 \\nworkers and thereafter one for every additional 50 workers. In addition\
      \ one \\nurinal accommodation shall be provided for every 100 workers. \\n \\n46.1.2 \\nWhen women are employed,\
      \ separate latrine and urinals accommodation shall be \\nprovided on the same scale as mentioned above. \\\
      n \\n46.1.3 \\nLatrine and urinals shall be provided as per Section 33 of BOCWA and maintained as \\nper Rule\
      \ 243 of BOCWR and shall also comply with the requirements of public health \\nauthorities \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.5599594116211,\n        669.9990844726562,\n        188.8888702392578,\n\
      \        692.9317016601562\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 7,\n      \"text\": \" \\n46.1.4 \\nMoving sites\
      \ \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.5599594116211,\n        697.8390502929688,\n       \
      \ 515.8886108398438,\n        784.1316528320312\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \ \\n46.1.4.1 In case of works like track laying, the zone of work is constantly moving at elevated \\nlevel\
      \ or at underground level. In such cases mobile toilets with proper facility to drain \\nthe sullage shall\
      \ be provided at reasonably accessible distance. \\n \\n46.1.5 \\nIn case if the contractor fail to provide\
      \ required number of urinals and latrines \\nor fail to maintain it as per the requirements of Public Health\
      \ laws, the Employer \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        39.599998474121094,\n        24.479991912841797,\n\

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"page_rect\": [\n\
      \    0.0,\n    0.0,\n    595.219970703125,\n    842.0\n  ],\n  \"pdf_page\": 70,\n  \"review_status\": \"\
      pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\\n  \
      \                                                        254\\n                  ii)   Keep noise to the lowest\
      \ reasonably practicable level.  Appropriate measures\\n                     will be taken to ensure that\
      \ construction works will not cause any unnecessary\\n                or excessive noise, which may disturb\
      \ the occupants of any nearby dwellings,\\n                schools, hospitals, or premises with similar sensitivity\
      \ to noise. Use equipment\\n                with  effective  noise-suppression  devices and employ  other\
      \  noise  control\\n             measures as to protect the public.\\n\\n                  iii)   Schedule\
      \ and conduct operations in a manner that will minimize, to the greatest\\n                extent  feasible,\
      \  the  disturbance  to  the  public  in  areas  adjacent  to  the\\n                construction  activities\
      \ and  to occupants  of  buildings  in the  vicinity  of the\\n                construction activities.\\\
      n\\n             iv)   The Contractor shall submit to the Employer a Noise Monitoring and Control\\n     \
      \         Plan (NMCP) under contract specific Site Environmental Plan.   It shall include\\n             \
      \          full and comprehensive details of all powered mechanical equipment, which he\\n              proposes\
      \ to use during daytime and night - time, and of his proposed working\\n             methods and noise level\
      \ reduction measures. The NMCP shall include detailed\\n               noise calculations and vibration levels\
      \ to demonstrate the anticipated noise\\n               generation and vibrations by the Contractor.\\n\\\
      n           v)   The NMCP prepared by the Contractor  shall guide the implementation  of\\n              \
      \  construction  activity. The NMCP  will be reviewed on a regular basis and\\n              updated  as \
      \ necessary  to  assure  that  current  construction  activities  are\\n               addressed.  It may\
      \ appear as a regular agenda item in project coordination\\n               meetings, if noise is an issue\
      \ at any location in the contract.\\n\\n\\n43.4     Occupational Noise\\n\\n\\n                i)     Protection\
      \  against  the  effects  of  occupational  noise exposure should be\\n               provided when the sound\
      \ level exceeds the threshold values as provided in\\n                Project SHE Manual.\\n\\n          \
      \        ii)   When employees are subjected to sound levels exceeding those listed in the\\n             \
      \   Table, feasible administrative or engineering controls should be utilized as given\\n                \
      \   in this document and BMRC’s Project SHE Manual.\\n\\n                  iii)      If such controls fail\
      \ to reduce sound levels within the levels of the table, personal\\n                 protective equipment\
      \ shall be provided and used to reduce sound levels within\\n                the levels of the table.\\n\\\
      n             iv)   When the daily noise exposure is composed of two or more periods of noise\\n         \
      \     exposure of different levels, their combined effect should be considered, rather\\n               than\
      \ the individual effect of each. Exposure to different levels for various periods\\n                 of time\
      \ shall be computed according to the formula and sample computations,\\n              as given in project\
      \ SHE Manual.\\n\\n\\n43.4      Vibration Level\\n\\n\\n43.4.1    In locations where the alignment  is close\
      \ to  historical  / heritage structures, the\\n          contractor shall prepare a monitoring scheme prior\
      \ to construction at such locations.\\n          This scheme for monitoring vibration level at such historical\
      \ / heritage sites shall be\\n         submitted to Employer for his approval. This scheme shall include:\\\
      n\\n                i)     Monitoring requirements  for  vibrations  at  regular  intervals  throughout  the\\\
      n                construction period.\\n\\n                  ii)    Pre-construction  structural  integrity\
      \  inspections  of  historic  and  sensitive\\n                structures in project activity.\\n\\n     \
      \             iii)    Information dissemination about the construction method, probable  effects,\\n     \
      \            quality control measures and precautions to be used.\\n\\n             iv)   The vibration level\
      \ limits at work sites adjacent to the alignment shall conform to\\n                the permitted values of\
      \ peak p velocity as given in article project SHE Manual.\\n\\n\\n44.0      Ventilation and illumination\\\
      n\\n\\n44.1      Ventilation\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.              September\
      \ 2026     Section Page 66 of 114\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        47.039981842041016,\n\
      \        15.768376350402832,\n        561.0993041992188,\n        24.210372924804688\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 0,\n      \"text\": \"                                                                                 \
      \                                                  Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual \\n\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.4399642944336,\n        26.57955551147461,\n        85.20714569091797,\n\
      \        35.93199157714844\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"bbox\": [\n        82.4399642944336,\n        809.042724609375,\n        508.6270751953125,\n   \
      \     830.5714111328125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation\
      \ Ltd.                              September 2026         Section Page 66 of 114 \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"bbox\": [\n        82.44075775146484,\n        38.09945297241211,\n        512.76806640625,\n   \
      \     341.4522705078125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"ii) \\nKeep noise to the\
      \ lowest reasonably practicable level.  Appropriate measures \\nwill be taken to ensure that construction\
      \ works will not cause any unnecessary \\nor excessive noise, which may disturb the occupants of any nearby\
      \ dwellings, \\nschools, hospitals, or premises with similar sensitivity to noise. Use equipment \\nwith effective\
      \ noise-suppression devices and employ other noise control \\nmeasures as to protect the public.   \\niii)\
      \ \\nSchedule and conduct operations in a manner that will minimize, to the greatest \\nextent feasible, the\
      \ disturbance to the public in areas adjacent to the \\nconstruction activities and to occupants of buildings\
      \ in the vicinity of the \\nconstruction activities. \\niv) \\nThe Contractor shall submit to the Employer\
      \ a Noise Monitoring and Control \\nPlan (NMCP) under contract specific Site Environmental Plan.  It shall\
      \ include \\nfull and comprehensive details of all powered mechanical equipment, which he \\nproposes to use\
      \ during daytime and night - time, and of his proposed working \\nmethods and noise level reduction measures.\
      \ The NMCP shall include detailed \\nnoise calculations and vibration levels to demonstrate the anticipated\
      \ noise \\ngeneration and vibrations by the Contractor.  \\nv) \\nThe NMCP prepared by the Contractor shall\
      \ guide the implementation of \\nconstruction activity. The NMCP will be reviewed on a regular basis and \\\
      nupdated as necessary to assure that current construction activities are \\naddressed. It may appear as a\
      \ regular agenda item in project coordination \\nmeetings, if noise is an issue at any location in the contract.\
      \ \\n \\n43.4  \\nOccupational Noise \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.4400863647461,\n\
      \        346.61981201171875,\n        512.7677001953125,\n        768.8919067382812\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 4,\n      \"text\": \"i) \\nProtection against the effects of occupational noise exposure should be \\nprovided\
      \ when the sound level exceeds the threshold values as provided in \\nProject SHE Manual. \\nii) \\nWhen employees\
      \ are subjected to sound levels exceeding those listed in the \\nTable, feasible administrative or engineering\
      \ controls should be utilized as given \\nin this document and BMRC’s Project SHE Manual. \\niii) \\nIf such\
      \ controls fail to reduce sound levels within the levels of the table, personal \\nprotective equipment shall\
      \ be provided and used to reduce sound levels within \\nthe levels of the table.  \\niv) \\nWhen the daily\
      \ noise exposure is composed of two or more periods of noise \\nexposure of different levels, their combined\
      \ effect should be considered, rather \\nthan the individual effect of each. Exposure to different levels\
      \ for various periods \\nof time shall be computed according to the formula and sample computations, \\nas\
      \ given in project SHE Manual.  \\n \\n43.4  \\nVibration Level  \\n \\n43.4.1  \\nIn locations where the\
      \ alignment is close to historical / heritage structures, the \\ncontractor shall prepare a monitoring scheme\
      \ prior to construction at such locations. \\nThis scheme for monitoring vibration level at such historical\
      \ / heritage sites shall be \\nsubmitted to Employer for his approval. This scheme shall include: \\ni) \\\
      nMonitoring requirements for vibrations at regular intervals throughout the \\nconstruction period. \\nii)\
      \ \\nPre-construction structural integrity inspections of historic and sensitive \\nstructures in project\
      \ activity. \\niii) \\nInformation dissemination about the construction method, probable effects, \\nquality\
      \ control measures and precautions to be used. \\niv) \\nThe vibration level limits at work sites adjacent\
      \ to the alignment shall conform to \\nthe permitted values of peak p velocity as given in article project\
      \ SHE Manual. \\n \\n44.0 \\nVentilation and illumination  \\n \\n44.1  \\nVentilation \\n\"\n    },\n   \
      \ {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        546.0439453125,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        570.0199584960938,\n\
      \        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"254\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n\
      }"

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"page_rect\": [\n\
      \    0.0,\n    0.0,\n    595.219970703125,\n    842.0\n  ],\n  \"pdf_page\": 67,\n  \"review_status\": \"\
      pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\\n251\\\
      n          43.1.3   The noise monitoring requirements including monitoring locations are given in the\\n \
      \                    project SHE Manual.\\n\\n\\n          43.2    NOISE MONITORING\\n\\n          43.2.1\
      \   The activities which are expected to cause noise during the construction of Bangalore\\n             \
      \      metro, include noise from construction equipment, construction activities such as portal\\n       \
      \              construction, boring for piling, earthwork excavation, concreting, viaduct, station, depot\\\
      n                    construction (including shifting of launching truss  / girder) and removal of spoil and\\\
      n                movement of construction vehicles and delivery vehicles, traveling to and from the\\n   \
      \                 construction and disposal sites.\\n          43.2.2   The level of impact of these noise\
      \ sources depends upon the noise characteristics of\\n                    the equipment and activities involved\
      \ the construction schedule, and the distance from\\n                   noise sensitive receptors.\\n\\n \
      \         43.2.3   The Noise Monitoring and Control Plan (NMCP) in contract specific site Environmental\\\
      n                    Quality Management Plan prepared by the Contractor shall establish procedures to\\n \
      \                  monitor construction noise and determine when to apply measures to control noise\\n   \
      \                   pollution due to construction activities at work sites.\\n\\n          43.2.4   The NMCP\
      \ will provide site description, define acceptable noise monitoring equipment,\\n                    provide\
      \ siting and operating procedures for noise equipment, indicate reports and record\\n                   keeping\
      \ on noise monitoring data.\\n\\n\\n\\n          43.2.5   The NMCP will provide guidance for construction\
      \ activity.   It shall also address noise\\n                  performance criteria used in the selection of\
      \ construction equipment.\\n\\n          43.2.6   The Noise Monitoring and Control Plan shall provide for:\\\
      n                       a) Definition of noise-sensitive uses in the zones affected by construction.\\n  \
      \                     b)   Calculation of future noise levels at the closest noise-sensitive receptors to\
      \ the\\n                            construction activity based on construction activity and ambient noise\
      \ levels.\\n                        c)   Evaluation and specification of the noise abatement measures that\
      \ can be\\n                           applied to meet the noise objectives.\\n                       d)  \
      \ Monitoring construction activity and providing adjustments to noise abatement\\n                       \
      \      controls that may be required to increase their effectiveness.\\n                       e)  regular\
      \ reporting\\n                                 f)   Requirements of NMCP:\\n                             \
      \               (i)     It shall specify the nighttime and daytime construction activities, monitoring\\n\
      \                                    locations, equipment, procedures, and schedule of measurements and\\\
      n                                   reporting methods to be used.\\n                                     \
      \           (ii)    It shall contain a scaled plan indicating monitoring locations, including\\n         \
      \                   measurements to be taken at construction site boundaries and at nearby\\n            \
      \                         residential zones.\\n                                                  (iii)  a\
      \ record of the noise characteristics of powered mechanical equipment\\n                             proposed\
      \ to be used during day time and night time and of proposed\\n                               working methods\
      \ and of potential noise level reduction measures.\\n                                      (iv)  Provisions\
      \ for immediate notification when measured noise levels exceed\\n                                 allowable\
      \ limits,\\n                                  (v)  Provide a reporting procedure whereby noise-monitoring\
      \ data is furnished\\n                                     to the Engineer on a weekly basis.\\n\\n      \
      \    43.2.7    In defining the requirements of the NMCP, available measures for noise control, such\\n   \
      \                 as, the use of equipment with special exhaust silencers or enclosures, and the\\n      \
      \              construction of temporary enclosures or noise barriers around specific construction\\n    \
      \                   site activity areas shall be considered.  It should also specify the measures to be\\\
      n                  adopted to counter the impact of noise pollution for public and workers working at site\\\
      n                    during construction.\\n\\n          43.2.8      If the measured noise levels exceed the\
      \ noise limits, the noise levels shall be reduced\\n                 by appropriate abatement measures.\\\
      n\\n         43.2.9    The NMCP will be reviewed on a regular basis and updated as necessary to assure\\n\
      \                    current construction activities are addressed.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n         Bangalore Metro\
      \ Rail Corporation Ltd.              September 2026     Section Page 63 of 114\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n\
      \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        50.15998077392578,\n        15.768376350402832,\n        564.21923828125,\n\
      \        24.210372924804688\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \"                    \
      \                                                                                                        \
      \       Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.5599594116211,\n\
      \        26.57955551147461,\n        88.32714080810547,\n        35.93199157714844\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 1,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.5599594116211,\n        809.042724609375,\n\
      \        511.7470703125,\n        830.5714111328125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                              September 2026         Section Page\
      \ 63 of 114 \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.55757904052734,\n        38.09945297241211,\n\
      \        520.326904296875,\n        366.6521911621094\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"43.1.3  \\nThe noise monitoring requirements including monitoring locations are given in the \\nproject\
      \ SHE Manual. \\n \\n43.2 \\nNOISE MONITORING \\n \\n43.2.1 \\nThe activities which are expected to cause\
      \ noise during the construction of Bangalore \\nmetro, include noise from construction equipment, construction\
      \ activities such as portal \\nconstruction, boring for piling, earthwork excavation, concreting, viaduct,\
      \ station, depot \\nconstruction (including shifting of launching truss / girder) and removal of spoil and\
      \ \\nmovement of construction vehicles and delivery vehicles, traveling to and from the \\nconstruction and\
      \ disposal sites. \\n43.2.2 \\nThe level of impact of these noise sources depends upon the noise characteristics\
      \ of \\nthe equipment and activities involved the construction schedule, and the distance from \\nnoise sensitive\
      \ receptors. \\n \\n43.2.3 \\nThe Noise Monitoring and Control Plan (NMCP) in contract specific site Environmental\
      \ \\nQuality Management Plan prepared by the Contractor shall establish procedures to \\nmonitor construction\
      \ noise and determine when to apply measures to control noise \\npollution due to construction activities\
      \ at work sites. \\n \\n43.2.4 \\nThe NMCP will provide site description, define acceptable noise monitoring\
      \ equipment, \\nprovide siting and operating procedures for noise equipment, indicate reports and record \\\
      nkeeping on noise monitoring data.  \\n \\n \\n43.2.5 \\nThe NMCP will provide guidance for construction activity.\
      \  It shall also address noise \\nperformance criteria used in the selection of construction equipment.  \\\
      n \\n43.2.6 \\nThe Noise Monitoring and Control Plan shall provide for: \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        139.55767822265625,\n        368.8197021484375,\n        515.8851318359375,\n        470.2120361328125\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"a) Definition of noise-sensitive uses in the zones\
      \ affected by construction. \\nb) \\nCalculation of future noise levels at the closest noise-sensitive receptors\
      \ to the \\nconstruction activity based on construction activity and ambient noise levels. \\nc) \\nEvaluation\
      \ and specification of the noise abatement measures that can be \\napplied to meet the noise objectives. \\\
      nd) \\nMonitoring construction activity and providing adjustments to noise abatement \\ncontrols that may\
      \ be required to increase their effectiveness. \\ne) regular reporting \\nf) \\nRequirements of NMCP: \\n\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        166.55697631835938,\n        472.37957763671875,\n        515.8848876953125,\n\
      \        550.73193359375\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"(i) \\nIt shall specify\
      \ the nighttime and daytime construction activities, monitoring \\nlocations, equipment, procedures, and schedule\
      \ of measurements and \\nreporting methods to be used. \\n(ii) \\nIt shall contain a scaled plan indicating\
      \ monitoring locations, including \\nmeasurements to be taken at construction site boundaries and at nearby\
      \ \\nresidential zones. \\n(iii) a record of the noise characteristics of powered mechanical equipment \\\
      n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        166.55697631835938,\n        552.7794189453125,\n        515.8843383789062,\n\
      \        585.1719360351562\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"proposed to be used during\
      \ day time and night time and of proposed \\nworking methods and of potential noise level reduction measures.\
      \  \\n(iv)  Provisions for immediate notification when measured noise levels exceed \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"bbox\": [\n        78.47779083251953,\n        587.3394165039062,\n        515.8851318359375,\n \
      \       780.65185546875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 7,\n      \"text\": \"allowable limits, \\n(v)\
      \ \\nProvide a reporting procedure whereby noise-monitoring data is furnished \\nto the Engineer on a weekly\
      \ basis.   \\n \\n43.2.7 \\nIn defining the requirements of the NMCP, available measures for noise control,\
      \ such \\nas, the use of equipment with special exhaust silencers or enclosures, and the \\nconstruction of\
      \ temporary enclosures or noise barriers around specific construction \\nsite activity areas shall be considered.\
      \ It should also specify the measures to be \\nadopted to counter the impact of noise pollution for public\
      \ and workers working at site \\nduring construction. \\n \\n43.2.8 \\nIf the measured noise levels exceed\
      \ the noise limits, the noise levels shall be reduced \\nby appropriate abatement measures. \\n \\n43.2.9\
      \     The NMCP will be reviewed on a regular basis and updated as necessary to assure \\ncurrent construction\
      \ activities are addressed. \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        39.599998474121094,\n   \
      \     24.479991912841797,\n        63.57600021362305,\n        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 8,\n      \"text\": \"251\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n}"

    quote: "{\n  \"extraction_method\": \"geometric_sort\",\n  \"has_extracted_text\": true,\n  \"page_rect\": [\n\
      \    0.0,\n    0.0,\n    595.219970703125,\n    842.0\n  ],\n  \"pdf_page\": 69,\n  \"review_status\": \"\
      pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\\n253\\\
      n          Pneumatic Tools                                          85\\n          Scraper               \
      \                                  85\\n            Tractor                                              \
      \    84\\n\\n\\n         Noise emission limits apply to equipment used at surface of the construction site\
      \ during Night\\n                    time hours of 9 p.m. to 6 a.m.\\n\\n      43.2.19     The adjustments\
      \ for close in equipment noise measurement shall\\n                 be made in accordance with Table-4.\\\
      n\\n                                    TABLE – 4\\n              ADJUSTMENTS FOR CLOSE-IN EQUIPMENT NOISE\
      \ MEASUREMENTS\\n                    (Measurement Values to be subtracted from Measured Sound)\\n        \
      \ Distance (Feet)                      Level to Estimate Sound Level at 50 Feet dB (A)\\n              19-21\
      \                                               8\\n              22-23                                  \
      \             7\\n              24-26                                               6\\n              27-29\
      \                                               5\\n              30-33                                  \
      \             4\\n              34-37                                               3\\n              38-42\
      \                                               2\\n              43-47                                  \
      \             1\\n              48-50                                               0\\n\\n              \
      \                         TABLE- 5.\\n                          CONSTRUCTION VIBRATION LIMITS\\n         \
      \                    VIBRATION TYPE AND PERMISSIBL\\n       AGGREGATE DURATION            LIMIT\\n       \
      \    Sustained (1 hr/day)                               0.01 in/sec (80 VdB re 10-6 in/sec)\\n           Transient\
      \ (<1 hr/day)                               0.03 in/sec (90 VdB re 10-6 in/sec)\\n           Transient (<10\
      \ min/day)                            0.10 in/sec (100 VdB re 10-6 in/sec)\\n\\n\\n          43.2.20  When\
      \  Diesel Generator (DG) Sets  are used  for  operation  of equipment and\\n                   machinery,\
      \ then ‘Standards and Guidelines  for  control  of Noise  Pollution from\\n                    Stationery\
      \ DG Sets’, under Environment (Protection) Act, 1986 shall apply.\\n         43.2.21  Should the impact monitoring\
      \ record noise levels which are:\\n                         indicative of a deteriorating situation such\
      \ that closer monitoring is reasonably\\n                            indicated, or\\n                    \
      \    when in the opinion of the Engineer additional measurements are required in\\n                     \
      \   view of deteriorating noise environment, then, the Engineer may require the\\n                       \
      \  Contractor to increase the frequency of impact monitoring at any one or more of\\n                    \
      \      the monitoring stations until the results indicate an improving and acceptable\\n                 \
      \            level of noise.\\n\\n        43.2..22    The Contractor  shall submit a copy  of monitoring \
      \ results. The  results should\\n                    represent  a statistical evaluation of data for evaluation\
      \ of trends and comparison\\n                      with noise emission standards.\\n\\n        43.2.23   Where\
      \ the Engineer determines that the recorded Noise level is significantly\\n                    greater than\
      \ the acceptable levels, the Engineer may direct the Contractor to\\n                   take effective remedial\
      \ measures including, but not limited to, reviewing noise\\n                  sources and modifying working\
      \ procedures.\\n\\n         43.2.23    The Contractor shall inform the Engineer of all steps taken to investigate\
      \ cause of\\n                  exceedance and immediate action taken to avoid further exceedance through\\\
      n                       written reports and proposals for action under an Event Contingency Plan.\\n\\n  \
      \        43.3       Control Requirements\\n\\n\\n          43.3.1     Construction material should be operated\
      \ and transported in such a manner as not to\\n                     create unnecessary noise as outlined below:\\\
      n\\n\\n                                 i)    Perform Work within the procedures outlined herein and comply\
      \ with applicable\\n                        codes,  regulations, and  standards  established by  the  Central\
      \ and  State\\n                     Government and their agencies.\\n\\n\\n\\n         Bangalore Metro Rail\
      \ Corporation Ltd.              September 2026     Section Page 65 of 114\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n\
      \      \"bbox\": [\n        50.15998077392578,\n        15.768376350402832,\n        564.21923828125,\n  \
      \      24.210372924804688\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \"                      \
      \                                                                                                        \
      \     Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        85.5599594116211,\n\
      \        26.57955551147461,\n        88.32714080810547,\n        35.93199157714844\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\

      : \"VIBRATION TYPE AND PERMISSIBL \\nAGGREGATE DURATION  \\n \\nLIMIT        \\nSustained (1 hr/day) \\n0.01\

      \ (<10 min/day) \\n 0.10 in/sec (100 VdB re 10-6 in/sec) \\n \\n43.2.20 \\nWhen Diesel Generator (DG) Sets\

      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \" \\nindicative of a deteriorating situation such\
      \ that closer monitoring is reasonably \\nindicated, or \\n \\nwhen in the opinion of the Engineer additional\
      \ measurements are required in \\nview of deteriorating noise environment, then, the Engineer may require\
      \ the \\nContractor to increase the frequency of impact monitoring at any one or more of \\nthe monitoring\
      \ stations until the results indicate an improving and acceptable \\nlevel of noise. \\n \\n43.2..22 \\nThe\
      \ Contractor shall submit a copy of monitoring results. The results should \\nrepresent  a statistical evaluation\
      \ of data for evaluation of trends and comparison \\nwith noise  emission standards. \\n \\n \\n43.2.23  \
      \  Where the Engineer determines that the recorded Noise level is significantly \\n  \\n     greater than\
      \ the acceptable levels, the Engineer may direct the Contractor to \\n  \\n     take effective remedial measures\
      \ including, but not limited to, reviewing noise \\n  \\n     sources and modifying working procedures. \\\
      n \\n43.2.23     The Contractor shall inform the Engineer of all steps taken to investigate cause of \\n \
      \ \\n     exceedance and immediate action taken to avoid further exceedance through  \\n  \\n     written\
      \ reports and proposals for action under an Event Contingency Plan. \\n43.3  \\n  Control Requirements \\\
      n \\n \\n43.3.1  \\nConstruction material should be operated and transported in such a manner as not to  \\\
      ncreate unnecessary noise as outlined below: \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        130.56024169921875,\n\
      \        755.5792236328125,\n        515.8878173828125,\n        787.9716796875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 10,\n      \"text\": \"i) \\nPerform Work within the procedures outlined herein and comply with applicable\

- kind: parameter
  text: Construction noise limits for residential areas are 75 dB(A) during daytime and 65 dB(A) during nighttime,
    85 dB(A) for commercial areas, and 90 dB(A) for industrial areas, measured at a distance of 200 feet from construction
    limits or the nearest affected building.
  evidence_ids:
